002316: function collectAutoStopEvidence(doc, status = '') {

002413: };
002414: }
002415: const explicitLack = /\b(?:auto-?ok\s+alert:\s*)?it\s+lacks?:\s*\d+\s+[^|]{1,180}/i.test(evidence);
002416: const missingRequirement = /\bmissing(?:\s+(?:vehicles?|personnel))?\s*:\s*[^|]{1,220}/i.test(evidence);
002417: const unavailableResource =
002418: /\b(?:no|not\s+enough)\s+(?:suitable\s+|available\s+)?(?:vehicle|vehicles|unit|units|ambulance|ambulances|officer|officers|helicopter|helicopters|appliance|appliances|engine|engines|truck|trucks|boat|boats|drone|drones|car|cars|crew|crews)\b/i.test(evidence) ||
002419: /\b(?:could\s+not|cannot|can['\u2019]?t|unable\s+to)\s+find\b/i.test(evidence) ||
002420: /\b(?:resource|vehicle|unit|appliance|ambulance|helicopter|officer)\b.{0,90}\b(?:unavailable|not\s+available|shortfall)\b/i.test(evidence);
002421: if (explicitLack || missingRequirement || unavailableResource) {
002422: return {
002423: matched: true,
002424: category: 'RESOURCE_SHORTAGE',
002425: reason: 'Required mission resource is unavailable',
002426: evidence,
002427: primary,
002428: terminal,
002429: };
002430: }
002431: return { matched: false, category: '', reason: '', evidence, primary, terminal: false };
002432: }

033492:             )
033493:             .replace(
033494:                 /\s+(?:Missing\s+Vehicles?|We\s+need)\s*:.+$/i,
033495:                 ''
033496:             )
033497:             .trim();
033498:     }
033499:     function getNamedPersonnelCount(
033500:         personnelSegment,
033501:         namePatternSource
033502:     ) {
033503:         const segment =
033504:             String(
033505:                 personnelSegment ||
033506:                 ''
033507:             );
033508:         if (
033509:             !segment ||
033510:             !namePatternSource
033511:         ) {
033512:             return 0;
033513:         }
033514:         const pattern =
033515:             new RegExp(
033516:                 `(?:\\b(\\d+)\\s*(?:x\\s*)?${namePatternSource}\\b|\\b${namePatternSource}\\b\\s*(?::|=|-)?\\s*(?:x\\s*)?(\\d+))`,
033517:                 'gi'
033518:             );
033519:         let maximum = 0;
033520:         let match;
033521:         while (
033522:             (
033523:                 match =
033524:                     pattern.exec(
033525:                         segment
033526:                     )
033527:             )
033528:         ) {
033529:             maximum =
033530:                 Math.max(
033531:                     maximum,
033532:                     parseInt(
033533:                         match[1] ||
033534:                         match[2],
033535:                         10
033536:                     ) ||
033537:                     0
033538:                 );
033539:         }
033540:         return maximum;
033541:     }
033542:     function shouldSkipLiveRequirement(name, value) {
033543:         const cleanedName = cleanRequirementName(name);
033544:         const text = `${cleanedName} ${value}`.toLowerCase();
033545:         const mappedName = resolveUnitName(cleanedName);
033546:         if (!cleanedName) return true;
033547:         if (shouldIgnoreRequiredMinimumRequirement(name) ||
033548:             shouldIgnoreRequiredMinimumRequirement(cleanedName)) return true;
033549:         if (!/^\d+$/.test(String(value || '').trim())) return true;
033550:         if (normaliseVehicleText(mappedName) === normaliseVehicleText('Ambulance x 01')) return true;
033551:         if (/^min\.?\s*pump\s*capacity$/i.test(cleanedName)) return true;
033552:         if (/^minimum\s*pump\s*capacity$/i.test(cleanedName)) return true;
033553:         if (/^pump\s*capacity$/i.test(cleanedName)) return true;
033554:         if (/^water\s*to\s*pump$/i.test(cleanedName)) return true;
033555:         if (/water\s*damage\s*pump\s*extension/i.test(cleanedName)) return true;
033556:         if (/\bextension\b/i.test(cleanedName) && !/vehicle|unit|officer|car|engine|pump$/i.test(cleanedName)) return true;
033557:         if (/only required/.test(text)) return true;
033558:         if (/^probability of /i.test(cleanedName)) return true;
033559:         if (/\bprobability\b/.test(text)) return true;
033560:         if (/personnel/.test(text)) return true;
033561:         if (/patients?/.test(text)) return true;
033562:         if (/prisoners?/.test(text)) return true;
033563:         if (/transport request/.test(text)) return true;
033564:         if (/chance/.test(text)) return true;
033565:         return false;
033566:     }
033567:     function findMissionRequirementTable(
033568:         doc,
033569:         suppliedTables = null
033570:     ) {
033571:         if (!doc) return null;
033572:         const tables = Array.isArray(suppliedTables)
033573:             ? suppliedTables
033574:             : Array.from(
033575:                 doc.querySelectorAll(
033576:                     'table.table, table'
033577:                 )
033578:             );
033579:         let bestTable = null;
033580:         let bestScore = 0;
033581:         const normalise = value => {
033582:             return String(value || '')
033583:                 .replace(/\s+/g, ' ')
033584:                 .trim();
033585:         };
033586:         tables.forEach(table => {
033587:             const headings = Array.from(
033588:                 table.querySelectorAll(
033589:                     'thead th, thead td, th'
033590:                 )
033591:             ).map(cell => normalise(
033592:                 cell.textContent
033593:             ));
033594:             const headingText =
033595:                 headings.join(' ');
033596:             let score = 0;
033597:             if (
033598:                 headings.some(heading =>
033599:                     /^Vehicle and Personnel Requirements$/i
033600:                         .test(heading)
033601:                 )
033602:             ) {
033603:                 score += 10000;
033604:             } else if (
033605:                 /Vehicle/i.test(headingText) &&
033606:                 /Personnel/i.test(headingText) &&
033607:                 /Requirements?/i.test(headingText)
033608:             ) {
033609:                 score += 8000;
033610:             }
033611:             let actionableRows = 0;
033612:             table.querySelectorAll(
033613:                 'tbody tr, tr'
033614:             ).forEach(row => {
033615:                 const cells = Array.from(
033616:                     row.querySelectorAll('td')
033617:                 ).map(cell => normalise(
033618:                     cell.textContent
033619:                 ));
033620:                 if (cells.length < 2) return;
033621:                 if (
033622:                     /^(?:Required\s+|Maximum amount of (?:cars|trucks) to tow|(?:Cars|Trucks) to tow)/i
033623:                         .test(cells[0]) &&
033624:                     /\d/.test(cells[1])
033625:                 ) {
033626:                     actionableRows += 1;
033627:                 }
033628:             });
033629:             if (actionableRows > 0) {
033630:                 score += 100 +
033631:                     actionableRows;
033632:             }
033633:             if (score > bestScore) {
033634:                 bestScore = score;
033635:                 bestTable = table;
033636:             }
033637:         });
033638:         return bestTable;
033639:     }
033640:     function extractMissionDefinitionRequiredPersonnelRows(
033641:         doc,
033642:         excludedTable = null
033643:     ) {
033644:         const rows = [];
033645:         const rawRows = [];
033646:         let requiredPersonnelRowFound = false;
033647:         if (!doc) {
033648:             return rows;
033649:         }
033650:         Array.from(
033651:             doc.querySelectorAll(
033652:                 'table tbody tr, table tr'
033653:             )
033654:         ).forEach(tr => {
033655:             if (
033656:                 excludedTable &&
033657:                 excludedTable.contains(tr)
033658:             ) {
033659:                 return;
033660:             }
033661:             const cells = Array.from(
033662:                 tr.querySelectorAll('td')
033663:             ).map(td => {
033664:                 return String(td.textContent || '')
033665:                     .replace(/\s+/g, ' ')
033666:                     .trim();
033667:             });
033668:             if (cells.length < 2) return;
033669:             const rawRequirementName = cells[0];
033670:             const cleanedName =
033671:                 cleanRequirementName(
033672:                     rawRequirementName
033673:                 );
033674:             if (
033675:                 !/^Personnel(?:\s+Requirements?)?$/i.test(
033676:                     cleanedName
033677:                 )
033678:             ) {
033679:                 return;
033680:             }
033681:             requiredPersonnelRowFound = true;
033682:             const amountText = String(cells[1] || '')
033683:                 .replace(/\s+/g, ' ')
033684:                 .trim();
033685:             rawRows.push({
033686:                 label: rawRequirementName,
033687:                 value: amountText
033688:             });
033689:             const trainedRequirements =
033690:                 getMissionDefinitionTrainedPersonnelRequirements(
033691:                     rawRequirementName,
033692:                     amountText
033693:                 );
033694:             const sarRequirements =
033695:                 getMissionDefinitionSarPersonnelVehicleRequirements(
033696:                     rawRequirementName,
033697:                     amountText
033698:                 );
033699:             if (trainedRequirements.length > 0) {
033700:                 rows.push({
033701:                     unitName:
033702:                         MF_TRAINED_PERSONNEL_ROW_NAME,
033703:                     stillNeeded:
033704:                         getTrainedPersonnelVehicleTarget(
033705:                             trainedRequirements
033706:                         ),
033707:                     isTrainedPersonnelRequirement:
033708:                         true,
033709:                     personnelTrainingRequirements:
033710:                         trainedRequirements,
033711:                     missionDefinitionRequiredPersonnel:
033712:                         true,
033713:                     source:
033714:                         'mission-definition-required-personnel'
033715:                 });
033716:             }
033717:             sarRequirements.forEach(conversion => {
033718:                 rows.push({
033719:                     unitName:
033720:                         conversion.unitName,
033721:                     stillNeeded:
033722:                         conversion.stillNeeded,
033723:                     personnelRequirement:
033724:                         conversion.personnelRequirement,
033725:                     missionDefinitionRequiredPersonnel:
033726:                         true,
033727:                     source:
033728:                         'mission-definition-required-personnel'
033729:                 });
033730:             });
033731:             if (
033732:                 mfDebugEnabled &&
033733:                 (
033734:                     trainedRequirements.length > 0 ||
033735:                     sarRequirements.length > 0
033736:                 )
033737:             ) {
033738:                 const parts = [];
033739:                 if (trainedRequirements.length > 0) {
033740:                     parts.push(
033741:                         formatTrainedPersonnelRequirements(
033742:                             trainedRequirements
033743:                         )
033744:                     );
033745:                 }
033746:                 sarRequirements.forEach(conversion => {
033747:                     parts.push(
033748:                         `${conversion.personnelRequirement} -> ${conversion.unitName} x${conversion.stillNeeded}`
033749:                     );
033750:                 });
033751:                 debugLog(
033752:                     'UNIT FINDER OTHER INFORMATION REQUIRED PERSONNEL',
033753:                     parts.join(' | ')
033754:                 );
033755:             }
033756:         });
033757:         try {
033758:             Object.defineProperties(
033759:                 rows,
033760:                 {
033761:                     missionDefinitionRequiredPersonnelFound: {
033762:                         value: requiredPersonnelRowFound,
033763:                         enumerable: false
033764:                     },
033765:                     rawMissionDefinitionRequiredPersonnelRows: {
033766:                         value: rawRows,
033767:                         enumerable: false
033768:                     }
033769:                 }
033770:             );
033771:         } catch (_error) {}
033772:         return rows;
033773:     }
033774:     function extractLiveMissionRequirementRows(html) {
033775:         const doc = new DOMParser().parseFromString(html, 'text/html');
033776:         const candidateTables =
033777:             Array.from(
033778:                 doc.querySelectorAll(
033779:                     'table.table, table'
033780:                 )
033781:             );
033782:         const table =
033783:             findMissionRequirementTable(
033784:                 doc,
033785:                 candidateTables
033786:             );
033787:         if (
033788:             mfDebugEnabled
033789:         ) {
033790:             debugLog(
033791:                 'UNIT FINDER ATTACHMENT TABLE',
033792:                 table
033793:                     ? 'Found exact Vehicle and Personnel Requirements table.'
033794:                     : `No exact Vehicle and Personnel Requirements table found among ${candidateTables.length} table(s).`
033795:             );
033796:         }
033797:         const rows = [];
033798:         mfLastMissionDefinitionRawRows = [];
033799:         if (table) {
033800:             mfLastMissionDefinitionRawRows = Array.from(
033801:                 table.querySelectorAll('tbody tr, tr')
033802:             ).map(tr => {
033803:                 const cells = Array.from(
033804:                     tr.querySelectorAll('td')
033805:                 );
033806:                 return {
033807:                     label: String(cells[0]?.textContent || '')
033808:                         .replace(/\s+/g, ' ')
033809:                         .trim(),
033810:                     value: String(cells[1]?.textContent || '')
033811:                         .replace(/\s+/g, ' ')
033812:                         .trim()
033813:                 };
033814:             }).filter(row => row.label || row.value);
033815:             table.querySelectorAll('tbody tr, tr').forEach(tr => {
033816:                 const cells = Array.from(tr.querySelectorAll('td')).map(td => td.textContent.replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim());
033817:                 if (cells.length < 2) return;
033818:                 const rawRequirementName = cells[0];
033819:                 const originalName = cleanRequirementName(rawRequirementName);
033820:                 const amountText = cells[1].replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim();
033821:                 if (
033822:                     shouldIgnoreRequiredMinimumRequirement(rawRequirementName) ||
033823:                     shouldIgnoreRequiredMinimumRequirement(originalName)
033824:                 ) {
033825:                     return;
033826:                 }
033827:                 if (mfDebugEnabled) {
033828:                     debugLog(
033829:                         'UNIT FINDER ATTACHMENT ROW',
033830:                         `${originalName || '(blank)'} | value=${amountText}`
033831:                     );
033832:                 }
033833:                 const missionDefinitionPersonnelRequirements =
033834:                     getMissionDefinitionTrainedPersonnelRequirements(
033835:                         rawRequirementName,
033836:                         amountText
033837:                     );
033838:                 const missionDefinitionSarPersonnelRequirements =
033839:                     getMissionDefinitionSarPersonnelVehicleRequirements(
033840:                         rawRequirementName,
033841:                         amountText
033842:                     );
033843:                 if (
033844:                     missionDefinitionPersonnelRequirements.length > 0 ||
033845:                     missionDefinitionSarPersonnelRequirements.length > 0
033846:                 ) {
033847:                     if (
033848:                         missionDefinitionPersonnelRequirements.length > 0
033849:                     ) {
033850:                         rows.push({
033851:                             unitName:
033852:                                 MF_TRAINED_PERSONNEL_ROW_NAME,
033853:                             stillNeeded:
033854:                                 getTrainedPersonnelVehicleTarget(
033855:                                     missionDefinitionPersonnelRequirements
033856:                                 ),
033857:                             isTrainedPersonnelRequirement:
033858:                                 true,
033859:                             personnelTrainingRequirements:
033860:                                 missionDefinitionPersonnelRequirements,
033861:                             missionDefinitionRequiredPersonnel:
033862:                                 true,
033863:                             source:
033864:                                 'mission-definition-required-personnel'
033865:                         });
033866:                     }
033867:                     missionDefinitionSarPersonnelRequirements
033868:                         .forEach(conversion => {
033869:                             rows.push({
033870:                                 unitName:
033871:                                     conversion.unitName,
033872:                                 stillNeeded:
033873:                                     conversion.stillNeeded,
033874:                                 personnelRequirement:
033875:                                     conversion.personnelRequirement,
033876:                                 missionDefinitionRequiredPersonnel:
033877:                                     true,
033878:                                 source:
033879:                                     'mission-definition-required-personnel'
033880:                             });
033881:                         });
033882:                     if (mfDebugEnabled) {
033883:                         const parts = [];
033884:                         if (
033885:                             missionDefinitionPersonnelRequirements.length > 0
033886:                         ) {
033887:                             parts.push(
033888:                                 formatTrainedPersonnelRequirements(
033889:                                     missionDefinitionPersonnelRequirements
033890:                                 )
033891:                             );
033892:                         }
033893:                         missionDefinitionSarPersonnelRequirements
033894:                             .forEach(conversion => {
033895:                                 parts.push(
033896:                                     `${conversion.personnelRequirement} -> ${conversion.unitName} x${conversion.stillNeeded}`
033897:                                 );
033898:                             });
033899:                         debugLog(
033900:                             'UNIT FINDER REQUIRED PERSONNEL',
033901:                             parts.join(' | ')
033902:                         );
033903:                     }
033904:                     return;
033905:                 }

033907:                     getCarsToTowVehicleRequirement(
033908:                         originalName,
033909:                         amountText
033910:                     );
033911:                 if (towRequirement) {
033912:                     rows.push({
033913:                         unitName:
033914:                             towRequirement.unitName,
033915:                         stillNeeded:
033916:                             towRequirement.stillNeeded,
033917:                         towCarsRequired:
033918:                             towRequirement.carsRequired
033919:                     });
033920:                     return;
033921:                 }
033922:                 if (
033923:                     isPoliceInspectorPersonnelRequirementName(
033924:                         originalName
033925:                     )
033926:                 ) {
033927:                     const inspectorRequired =
033928:                         Math.max(
033929:                             0,
033930:                             parseInt(
033931:                                 amountText,
033932:                                 10
033933:                             ) || 0
033934:                         );
033935:                     const inspectorRequirements =
033936:                         normalisePublicOrderTrainedRequirements([
033937:                             {
033938:                                 code:
033939:                                     'police_inspector',
033940:                                 label:
033941:                                     'Police Inspector',
033942:                                 required:
033943:                                     inspectorRequired
033944:                             }
033945:                         ]);
033946:                     if (
033947:                         inspectorRequirements.length >
033948:                         0
033949:                     ) {
033950:                         rows.push({
033951:                             unitName:
033952:                                 MF_TRAINED_PERSONNEL_ROW_NAME,
033953:                             stillNeeded:
033954:                                 getTrainedPersonnelVehicleTarget(
033955:                                     inspectorRequirements
033956:                                 ),
033957:                             isTrainedPersonnelRequirement:
033958:                                 true,
033959:                             personnelTrainingRequirements:
033960:                                 inspectorRequirements
033961:                         });
033962:                     }
033963:                     return;
033964:                 }
033965:                 const armedResponseRequirements =
033966:                     getArmedResponsePersonnelRequirements(
033967:                         originalName,
033968:                         amountText
033969:                     );
033970:                 if (
033971:                     armedResponseRequirements.length >
033972:                     0
033973:                 ) {
033974:                     rows.push({
033975:                         unitName:
033976:                             MF_TRAINED_PERSONNEL_ROW_NAME,
033977:                         stillNeeded:
033978:                             getTrainedPersonnelVehicleTarget(

033980:                             ),
033981:                         isTrainedPersonnelRequirement:
033982:                             true,
033983:                         personnelTrainingRequirements:

033985:                     });
033986:                     if (mfDebugEnabled) {
033987:                         debugLog(
033988:                             'UNIT FINDER ARMED PERSONNEL',
033989:                             `${originalName} x${amountText} -> exact type-25 Armed Traffic Car selection | ${formatTrainedPersonnelRequirements(armedResponseRequirements)}`
033990:                         );
033991:                     }
033992:                     return;
033993:                 }
033994:                 const sarPersonnelConversion =
033995:                     getSarPersonnelVehicleRequirement(
033996:                         originalName,
033997:                         amountText
033998:                     );
033999:                 if (sarPersonnelConversion) {
034000:                     rows.push({
034001:                         unitName:
034002:                             sarPersonnelConversion.unitName,
034003:                         stillNeeded:
034004:                             sarPersonnelConversion.stillNeeded,
034005:                         personnelRequirement:
034006:                             sarPersonnelConversion.personnelRequirement
034007:                     });
034008:                     if (mfDebugEnabled) {
034009:                         debugLog(
034010:                             'UNIT FINDER SAR PERSONNEL',
034011:                             `${sarPersonnelConversion.personnelRequirement} -> ${sarPersonnelConversion.unitName} x${sarPersonnelConversion.stillNeeded} (${sarPersonnelConversion.personnelPerVehicle} personnel per vehicle)`
034012:                         );
034013:                     }
034014:                     return;
034015:                 }
034016:                 if (shouldSkipLiveRequirement(originalName, amountText)) {
034017:                     if (
034018:                         mfDebugEnabled
034019:                     ) {
034020:                         debugLog(
034021:                             'UNIT FINDER ATTACHMENT SKIP',
034022:                             `${originalName || '(blank)'} | value=${amountText}`
034023:                         );
034024:                     }
034025:                     return;
034026:                 }
034027:                 const stillNeeded = parseInt(amountText, 10);
034028:                 if (!Number.isFinite(stillNeeded) || stillNeeded <= 0) return;
034029:                 rows.push({
034030:                     unitName: originalName,
034031:                     stillNeeded
034032:                 });
034033:                 if (mfDebugEnabled) {
034034:                     debugLog(
034035:                         'UNIT FINDER ATTACHMENT ACCEPT',
034036:                         `${originalName} x${stillNeeded} -> ${resolveUnitName(originalName)}`
034037:                     );
034038:                 }
034039:             });
034040:         }
034041:         const supplementalPersonnelRows =
034042:             extractMissionDefinitionRequiredPersonnelRows(
034043:                 doc,
034044:                 table
034045:             );
034046:         supplementalPersonnelRows.forEach(row => {
034047:             rows.push(row);
034048:         });
034049:         if (
034050:             Array.isArray(
034051:                 supplementalPersonnelRows
034052:                     .rawMissionDefinitionRequiredPersonnelRows
034053:             )
034054:         ) {
034055:             mfLastMissionDefinitionRawRows.push(
034056:                 ...supplementalPersonnelRows
034057:                     .rawMissionDefinitionRequiredPersonnelRows
034058:             );
034059:         }
034060:         extractTowCarRequirementRows(doc).forEach(row => rows.push(row));
034061:         extractTowHgvRequirementRows(doc).forEach(row => rows.push(row));
034062:         const mergedRows =
034063:             mergeRequirementRows(rows);
034064:         try {
034065:             Object.defineProperties(
034066:                 mergedRows,
034067:                 {
034068:                     missionRequirementTableFound: {
034069:                         value: Boolean(
034070:                             table ||
034071:                             supplementalPersonnelRows
034072:                                 .missionDefinitionRequiredPersonnelFound
034073:                         ),
034074:                         enumerable: false
034075:                     },
034076:                     missionRequirementTableCount: {
034077:                         value: candidateTables.length,
034078:                         enumerable: false
034079:                     }
034080:                 }
034081:             );
034082:         } catch (_error) {}
034083:         return mergedRows;
034084:     }
034085:     function extractTowCarRequirementRows(doc) {
034086:         const rows = [];
034087:         let maximumCarsToTow = 0;
034088:         doc.querySelectorAll('table.table, table').forEach(table => {
034089:             table.querySelectorAll('tbody tr, tr').forEach(tr => {
034090:                 const cells = Array.from(tr.querySelectorAll('td')).map(td => td.textContent.replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim());
034091:                 if (cells.length < 2) return;
034092:                 const label = cleanRequirementName(cells[0]);
034093:                 const valueText = cells[1].replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim();
034094:                 if (!/^Maximum amount of cars to tow$/i.test(label)) return;
034095:                 const cars = parseInt(valueText, 10);
034096:                 if (Number.isFinite(cars) && cars > maximumCarsToTow) {
034097:                     maximumCarsToTow = cars;
034098:                 }
034099:             });
034100:         });
034101:         if (maximumCarsToTow > 0) {
034102:             const flatbedsNeeded = maximumCarsToTow;
034103:             rows.push({
034104:                 unitName: 'Cars to tow',
034105:                 stillNeeded: flatbedsNeeded
034106:             });
034107:             if (mfDebugEnabled) {
034108:                 debugLog('LIVE TOW', `Maximum cars to tow=${maximumCarsToTow} -> Flatbed Recovery Vehicle x${flatbedsNeeded}`);
034109:             }
034110:         }
034111:         return rows;
034112:     }
034113:     function extractTowHgvRequirementRows(doc) {
034114:         const rows = [];
034115:         let maximumTrucksToTow = 0;
034116:         doc.querySelectorAll('table.table, table').forEach(table => {
034117:             table.querySelectorAll('tbody tr, tr').forEach(tr => {
034118:                 const cells = Array.from(tr.querySelectorAll('td')).map(td =>
034119:                     td.textContent.replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim()
034120:                 );
034121:                 if (cells.length < 2) return;
034122:                 const label = cleanRequirementName(cells[0]);
034123:                 const valueText = cells[1].replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim();
034124:                 if (!/^Maximum amount of trucks to tow$/i.test(label)) return;
034125:                 const trucks = parseInt(valueText, 10);
034126:                 if (Number.isFinite(trucks) && trucks > maximumTrucksToTow) {
034127:                     maximumTrucksToTow = trucks;
034128:                 }
034129:             });
034130:         });
034131:         if (maximumTrucksToTow > 0) {
034132:             rows.push({
034133:                 unitName: 'Trucks to tow',
034134:                 stillNeeded: maximumTrucksToTow
034135:             });
034136:             if (mfDebugEnabled) {
034137:                 debugLog(
034138:                     'LIVE HGV TOW',
034139:                     `Maximum trucks to tow=${maximumTrucksToTow} -> HGV Recovery Vehicle x${maximumTrucksToTow}`
034140:                 );
034141:             }
034142:         }

039108:             source === 'visible-missing-vehicles-alert' ||
039109:             source === 'visible-structured-missing-vehicles'
039110:         );
039111:     }
039112:     function isExplicitMissingPersonnelRequirementRow(item) {
039113:         const details =
039114:             item?.liveRequirementDetails ||
039115:             null;
039116:         const source = String(
039117:             item?.updateSource ||
039118:             item?.source ||
039119:             ''
039120:         ).trim();
039121:         return !!(
039122:             details?.explicitMissingPersonnel ||
039123:             source === 'visible-missing-personnel-alert'
039124:         );
039125:     }
039126:     function getMissionUpdateRowAuthority(item) {
039127:         if (
039128:             isExplicitMissingVehicleRequirementRow(item) ||
039129:             isExplicitMissingPersonnelRequirementRow(item)
039130:         ) {
039131:             return 300;
039132:         }
039133:         if (
039134:             item?.liveRequirementDetails?.dispatchTargetMode ===
039135:             'shortage'
039136:         ) {
039137:             return 200;
039138:         }
039139:         if (item?.isPatientAlertFallback) {
039140:             return 150;
039141:         }
039142:         if (item?.patientRequirementType) {
039143:             return 100;
039144:         }
039145:         return 0;
039146:     }
039147:     function hasExplicitCurrentMissingRequirementRows(rows) {
039148:         return (Array.isArray(rows) ? rows : []).some(item => {
039149:             return (
039150:                 isExplicitMissingVehicleRequirementRow(item) ||
039151:                 isExplicitMissingPersonnelRequirementRow(item)
039152:             );
039153:         });
039154:     }
039155:     function getExplicitCurrentMissingRequirementRows(rows) {
039156:         return (Array.isArray(rows) ? rows : []).filter(item => {
039157:             return (
039158:                 isExplicitMissingVehicleRequirementRow(item) ||
039159:                 isExplicitMissingPersonnelRequirementRow(item)
039160:             );
039161:         });
039162:     }
039163:     async function processRequirementRows(
039164:         requirementRows,

039200:             hasExplicitCurrentMissingRequirementRows(

039429:                     unitText,

039440:             );

039445:                 )

039464:                 ? 'assigned'

039473:                 visualSelected
039474:             );

039476:             if (visualSelected < effectiveRequired) {

039478:                     unitName: unitText,
039479:                     stillNeeded: effectiveRequired - visualSelected,
039480:                     requiredTotal: effectiveRequired
039481:                 });
039482:             }

039485:         if (mfStaffingBlockActive) {
039486:             changeDispatchBoxColor(false);
039487:             return false;
039488:         }
039489:         if (trainedPersonnelWarnings.length > 0) {
039490:             const warningText =
039491:                 trainedPersonnelWarnings.join('\n');
039492:             blockTrainedPersonnelDispatch(
039493:                 trainedPersonnelWarnings,
039494:                 'Unit Finder'
039495:             );
039496:             console.warn(
039497:                 '[Mission Finder] Verified trained-personnel shortage blocked dispatch:',
039498:                 warningText
039499:             );
039500:             if (!autoModeRunning) {
039501:                 originalAlert.call(
039502:                     window,
039503:                     `Verified trained-personnel shortage — dispatch blocked:\n\n${warningText}`
039504:                 );
039505:             }
039506:             return false;
039507:         }
039508:         if (trainedVehicleMissing.length > 0) {
039509:             changeDispatchBoxColor(false);
039510:             updateStatusBox(
039511:                 'Compatible trained-personnel vehicle capacity is still missing.'
039512:             );
039513:             originalAlert.call(
039514:                 window,
039515:                 `Final missing units:\n\n${trainedVehicleMissing.join('\n')}`
039516:             );
039517:             return false;
039518:         }
039519:         if (missingUnits.length > 0) {
039520:             return await retryMissingUnits(
039521:                 missingUnits
039522:             );
039523:         }
039524:         changeDispatchBoxColor(true);
039525:         return true;
039526:     }
039527:     async function processVehicles(
039528:         vehicleList,
039529:         options = {}
039530:     ) {
039531:         const listItems = vehicleList.querySelectorAll('li.class-x');
039532:         const requirementRows = [];
039533:         listItems.forEach(item => {
039534:             const unitText = [...item.childNodes]
039535:                 .filter(node => node.nodeType === Node.TEXT_NODE)
039536:                 .map(node => node.textContent.trim())[0];
039537:             if (!unitText) return;
039538:             const requiredAmount = parseInt(item.getAttribute('data-amount'), 10);
039539:             if (!Number.isFinite(requiredAmount) || requiredAmount <= 0) return;
039540:             requirementRows.push({
039541:                 unitName: unitText,
039542:                 stillNeeded: requiredAmount
039543:             });
039544:         });
039545:         return processRequirementRows(
039546:             requirementRows,
039547:             'fallback vehicle list',
039548:             options
039549:         );

039551:     async function retryMissingUnits(missingUnits) {

040801:     function handleUnitFinderPatientRequirements() {

041838:                 'Dispatch & Share button not found.'
041839:             );
041840:         }
041841:     }
041842:     function getGenericMissingVehicleRowsFromText(
041843:         text
041844:     ) {
041845:         const input =
041846:             String(
041847:                 text ||
041848:                 ''
041849:             )
041850:                 .replace(/\u00a0/g, ' ')
041851:                 .replace(
041852:                     /\s+/g,
041853:                     ' '
041854:                 )
041855:                 .trim();
041856:         const marker =
041857:             input.match(
041858:                 /Missing\s+Vehicles?\s*:\s*(.+)$/i
041859:             );
041860:         if (!marker) return [];
041861:         const requirementText =
041862:             marker[1]
041863:                 .replace(
041864:                     /\s+(?:Missing\s+Personnel|We\s+need)\s*:.+$/i,
041865:                     ''
041866:                 )
041867:                 .trim();
041868:         const rows = [];
041869:         const requirementRegex =
041870:             /(\d+)\s*(?:x\s*)?([A-Za-z][A-Za-z0-9 /&()'’\-]+?)(?=,\s*\d+\s*(?:x\s*)?|\s+and\s+\d+\s*(?:x\s*)?|$)/gi;
041871:         let match;
041872:         while (
041873:             (
041874:                 match =
041875:                     requirementRegex.exec(
041876:                         requirementText
041877:                     )
041878:             )
041879:         ) {
041880:             const amount =
041881:                 parseInt(
041882:                     match[1],
041883:                     10
041884:                 );
041885:             const name =
041886:                 String(
041887:                     match[2] ||
041888:                     ''
041889:                 )
041890:                     .replace(
041891:                         /\s+/g,
041892:                         ' '
041893:                     )
041894:                     .trim();
041895:             if (
041896:                 !Number.isFinite(amount) ||
041897:                 amount <= 0 ||
041898:                 !name
041899:             ) {
041900:                 continue;
041901:             }
041902:             if (
041903:                 /Police\s+Helicopter(?:s)?\s+or\s+Drone(?:s)?/i.test(
041904:                     name
041905:                 ) ||
041906:                 /Seagoing\s+Vessel/i.test(
041907:                     name
041908:                 ) ||
041909:                 /car(?:s)?\s+to\s+tow/i.test(
041910:                     name
041911:                 )
041912:             ) {
041913:                 continue;
041914:             }
041915:             rows.push({
041916:                 unitName:
041917:                     name,
041918:                 stillNeeded:
041919:                     amount
041920:             });
041921:         }
041922:         return rows;
041923:     }
041924:     function isLiveMissionRequirementsTable(
041925:         table
041926:     ) {
041927:         if (!table) {
041928:             return false;
041929:         }
041930:         return !!(
041931:             table.matches?.(
041932:                 'table[aria-label="Live mission requirements"]'
041933:             ) &&
041934:             table.closest?.(
041935:                 '#mc-map-command-toolkit-mission-requirements[data-mcms-requirements-panel="1"], ' +
041936:                 'section[data-mcms-requirements-panel="1"]'
041937:             )
041938:         );
041939:     }

045455:     function parseMissionIdFromHrefForQueueRestart(href) {
045456:         const value = String(href || '');
045457:         const directMatch = value.match(/\/missions\/(\d+)/i);
045458:         if (directMatch) return directMatch[1];
045459:         try {
045460:             const url = new URL(value, window.location.origin);
045461:             const missionId = url.searchParams.get('mission_id');
045462:             if (missionId) return missionId;
045463:         } catch (error) {}
045464:         const anyMatch = value.match(/mission_id=(\d+)/i);
045465:         return anyMatch ? anyMatch[1] : '';
045466:     }

046140:     function getVisibleInlineProblemAlertText() {
046141:         const scopes = getCurrentMissionAlertScopes();
046142:         if (!scopes.length) return '';
046143:         const alerts = [];
046144:         scopes.forEach(scope => {
046145:             scope.querySelectorAll(
046146:                 '#missing_text.alert-danger, .alert-missing-vehicles, .alert-danger[id^="missing_"], .alert-danger'
046147:             ).forEach(alert => {
046148:                 if (!alerts.includes(alert)) alerts.push(alert);
046149:             });
046150:         });
046151:         for (const alert of alerts) {
046152:             try {
046153:                 if (!isElementVisible(alert)) continue;
046154:             } catch (error) {}
046155:             const text = (alert.innerText || alert.textContent || '').replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim();
046156:             if (!text) continue;
046157:             const lower = text.toLowerCase();
046158:             if (
046159:                 lower.includes('missing personnel:') &&
046160:                 hasSupportedMissingPersonnelUpdate(text) &&
046161:                 !lower.includes('missing vehicles:')
046162:             ) {
046163:                 continue;
046164:             }
046165:             const isActionableMissionUpdateAlert =
046166:                 lower.includes(
046167:                     'missing vehicles:'
046168:                 ) ||
046169:                 lower.includes(
046170:                     'missing vehicle:'
046171:                 ) ||
046172:                 lower.includes(
046173:                     'we need:'
046174:                 ) ||
046175:                 (
046176:                     lower.includes(
046177:                         'missing personnel:'
046178:                     ) &&
046179:                     hasSupportedMissingPersonnelUpdate(
046180:                         text
046181:                     )
046182:                 );
046183:             if (
046184:                 isActionableMissionUpdateAlert &&
046185:                 areCurrentMissionUpdateRowsFullySelected()
046186:             ) {
046187:                 continue;
046188:             }
046189:             if (
046190:                 lower.includes('missing vehicles:') ||
046191:                 lower.includes('missing vehicle:') ||
046192:                 (
046193:                     lower.includes('missing personnel:') &&
046194:                     !hasSupportedMissingPersonnelUpdate(text)
046195:                 ) ||
046196:                 lower.includes('we need:') ||
046197:                 lower.includes('something went wrong') ||
046198:                 lower.includes('has not enough personal') ||
046199:                 lower.includes('has not enough personnel') ||
046200:                 lower.includes('right qualification')
046201:             ) {
046202:                 return text;
046203:             }
046204:         }
046205:         return '';
046206:     }
046207:     function normaliseVisibleRequirementName(rawName) {

051081:                 updateStatusBox(
051082:                     'Auto Mode: blocked a stale Dispatch. Running Unit Finder on the current mission...'
051083:                 );
051084:                 await wait(250);
051085:                 continue;
051086:             }
051087:             const prvSrvSelectionConfirmed =
051088:                 await confirmPrvSrvSelectionBeforeDispatch(
051089:                     'Auto Mode final dispatch decision'
051090:                 );
051091:             if (!prvSrvSelectionConfirmed) {
051092:                 const blockText = String(
051093:                     mfPrvSrvSelectionBlockText ||
051094:                     'Confirmed PRV/SRV units are still missing.'
051095:                 ).trim();
051096:                 stopAutoMode(
051097:                     `Auto stopped: ${blockText} Dispatch was not clicked.`
051098:                 );
051099:                 break;
051100:             }
051101:             if (!vehicleLoadState.ready) {
051102:                 const staffingBlocked =
051103:                     !!personnelQualificationAlert ||
051104:                     mfStaffingBlockActive ||
051105:                     vehicleLoadState.trainedPersonnelBlocked === true;
051106:                 if (staffingBlocked) {
051107:                     const blockText =
051108:                         String(
051109:                             vehicleLoadState.trainedPersonnelBlockText ||
051110:                             mfStaffingBlockText ||
051111:                             personnelQualificationAlert ||
051112:                             'Verified trained personnel are still missing.'
051113:                         ).trim();
051114:                     addMissionLogEntry(
051115:                         'skipped',
051116:                         getCurrentMissionName(),
051117:                         'Verified trained-personnel shortage; dispatch blocked',
051118:                         null
051119:                     );
051120:                     stopAutoMode(
051121:                         `Auto stopped: ${blockText} Dispatch was not clicked.`
051122:                     );
051123:                     break;
051124:                 }
051125:                 updateStatusBox(
051126:                     'Auto Mode: units not ready. Dispatching to skip mission...'
051127:                 );
051128:                 addMissionLogEntry(
051129:                     'skipped',
051130:                     getCurrentMissionName(),
051131:                     'Units not ready',
051132:                     null
051133:                 );
051134:                 if (
051135:                     getCurrentAutoDispatchSelectionState()
051136:                         .selectedCount === 0
051137:                 ) {
051138:                     stopAutoMode(
051139:                         'Auto stopped: Unit Finder selected 0 vehicles after a full-list retry. The mission was not dispatched.'
051140:                     );
051141:                     break;
051142:                 }
051143:                 const queueStateBeforeDispatch = getNextMissionQueueState();
051144:                 const finalQueueMission = isFinalQueueSignal(queueStateBeforeDispatch);
051145:                 logQueueState('skip-before-dispatch', queueStateBeforeDispatch, finalQueueMission);
051146:                 if (!claimAutoMissionDispatch(autoCycleMissionId)) {
051147:                     updateStatusBox(
051148:                         'Auto Mode: duplicate Dispatch blocked for this mission. Waiting for MissionChief to finish the existing handoff...'
051149:                     );
051150:                     break;
051151:                 }
051152:                 clearAutoAdvanceAfterDispatchState(
051153:                     'Auto skip uses native Dispatch-and-next'
051154:                 );
051155:                 if (finalQueueMission) {
051156:                     sessionStorage.setItem(
051157:                         MF_FINAL_QUEUE_DISPATCH_FLAG,
051158:                         'true'
051159:                     );
051160:                 }
051161:                 if (!isCurrentMissionExecutionOwner('before skip dispatch')) {
051162:                     removeMissionFinderPanelForClosedMission(
051163:                         'stale Auto Mode skip dispatch blocked'
051164:                     );
051165:                     break;
051166:                 }
051167:                 if (!clickDispatchOnly()) {
051168:                     releaseAutoMissionDispatch(autoCycleMissionId);
051169:                     sessionStorage.removeItem(
051170:                         MF_FINAL_QUEUE_DISPATCH_FLAG
051171:                     );
051172:                     stopAutoMode(
051173:                         finalQueueMission
051174:                             ? 'Auto stopped. Final mission Dispatch button was not found.'
051175:                             : 'Auto stopped. Dispatch & Next button was not found while skipping.'
051176:                     );
051177:                     break;
051178:                 }
051179:                 clearAutoSelectionMissionGuard(
051180:                     'skip mission dispatched'
051181:                 );
051182:                 resetVehicleLoadState();
051183:                 if (finalQueueMission) {
051184:                     const restarted =
051185:                         await handleAfterFinalQueueDispatch(
051186:                             queueStateBeforeDispatch
051187:                         );
051188:                     if (restarted) {
051189:                         continue;
051190:                     }
051191:                     break;
051192:                 }
051193:                 updateStatusBox(
051194:                     'Auto Mode: Dispatch & Next clicked. Waiting for the next mission...'
051195:                 );
051196:                 break;
051197:             }
051198:             updateStatusBox('Auto Mode dispatching mission...');
051199:             const missionName = getCurrentMissionName();
051200:             const missionCredits = getMissionCredits();
051201:             const queueStateBeforeDispatch = getNextMissionQueueState();
051202:             const finalQueueMission = isFinalQueueSignal(queueStateBeforeDispatch);
051203:             logQueueState('complete-before-dispatch', queueStateBeforeDispatch, finalQueueMission);
051204:             if (
051205:                 !await confirmPrvSrvSelectionBeforeDispatch(
051206:                     'Auto Mode immediate pre-dispatch recheck'
051207:                 )
051208:             ) {
051209:                 stopAutoMode(
051210:                     `Auto stopped: ${mfPrvSrvSelectionBlockText || 'Confirmed PRV/SRV units are still missing.'} Dispatch was not clicked.`
051211:                 );
051212:                 break;
051213:             }
051214:             if (!claimAutoMissionDispatch(autoCycleMissionId)) {
051215:                 updateStatusBox(
051216:                     'Auto Mode: duplicate Dispatch blocked for this mission. Waiting for MissionChief to finish the existing handoff...'
051217:                 );
051218:                 break;
051219:             }
051220:             clearAutoAdvanceAfterDispatchState(
051221:                 'Auto completed mission dispatch'
051222:             );
051223:             if (finalQueueMission) {
051224:                 sessionStorage.setItem(
051225:                     MF_FINAL_QUEUE_DISPATCH_FLAG,
051226:                     'true'
051227:                 );
051228:             }
051229:             if (!isCurrentMissionExecutionOwner('before completed dispatch')) {
051230:                 removeMissionFinderPanelForClosedMission(
051231:                     'stale Auto Mode completed dispatch blocked'
051232:                 );
051233:                 break;
051234:             }
051235:             const dispatchResult =
051236:                 clickMissionDispatchByValue(
051237:                     missionCredits,
051238:                     'Auto Mode completed mission'
051239:                 );
051240:             if (!dispatchResult.clicked) {
051241:                 releaseAutoMissionDispatch(autoCycleMissionId);
051242:                 sessionStorage.removeItem(
051243:                     MF_FINAL_QUEUE_DISPATCH_FLAG
051244:                 );
051245:                 stopAutoMode(
051246:                     finalQueueMission
051247:                         ? 'Auto stopped: final mission Dispatch button was not found.'
051248:                         : 'Auto stopped: Dispatch & Next button was not found.'
051249:                 );
051250:                 break;
051251:             }
051252:             clearAutoSelectionMissionGuard(
051253:                 'completed mission dispatched'
051254:             );
051255:             addMissionLogEntry(
051256:                 'completed',
051257:                 missionName,
051258:                 dispatchResult.shared
051259:                     ? 'Dispatched and shared'
051260:                     : '',
051261:                 missionCredits
051262:             );
051263:             resetVehicleLoadState();
051264:             updateStatusBox(
051265:                 dispatchResult.shared
051266:                     ? 'Auto Mode: Dispatch & Share clicked. Waiting for the next mission...'
051267:                     : finalQueueMission
051268:                         ? 'Auto Mode: final mission dispatched. Restarting the queue...'
051269:                         : 'Auto Mode: Dispatch & Next clicked. Waiting for the next mission...'
051270:             );
051271:             if (finalQueueMission) {
051272:                 const restarted =
051273:                     await handleAfterFinalQueueDispatch(
051274:                         queueStateBeforeDispatch
051275:                     );
051276:                 if (restarted) {
051277:                     continue;
051278:                 }
051279:                 break;
051280:             }
051281:             break;
051282:         }
051283:         autoModeLoopActive = false;
051284:         updateAutoModeButton();
051285:     }
051286:     function initialize() {
051287:         if (/^\/missions\/\d+\/gefangene\/entlassen\/?$/i.test(String(globalThis.location?.pathname || ''))) return;
051288:         if (typeof window !== 'undefined' && String(window.name || '').startsWith('mcn-v3-active-worker-transport-b-')) return;
051289:         globalThis.__MCN_BOOT_MARK__?.('mission-initialize-entered', document.readyState);
051290:         if (!isMissionPage()) return;
051291:         if (!claimCurrentMissionExecutionOwnership('initialize')) {
051292:             document.getElementById('mission-finder-wrapper')?.remove();
051293:             return;
051294:         }
051295:         synchroniseMissionInstanceState('initialize');
051296:         cleanupDuplicatePanels();
051297:         scheduleMissionFinderIphoneNativePickerSync(
051298:             'initialize',
051299:             0,
051300:             0
051301:         );
051302:         if (document.getElementById('mission-finder-wrapper')) {
051303:             if (readAllyStealPendingState()) {
051304:                 setTimeout(
051305:                     function() {
051306:                         resumeAllyStealAfterDispatchRefresh(
051307:                             'existing initialized panel'
051308:                         );
051309:                     },
051310:                     150
051311:                 );
051312:             } else if (
051313:                 readAutoAdvanceAfterDispatchState()
051314:             ) {
051315:                 setTimeout(
051316:                     function() {
051317:                         resumeAutoAdvanceAfterDispatch(
051318:                             'existing initialized panel'
051319:                         );
051320:                     },
051321:                     150
051322:                 );
051323:             }
051324:             return;
051325:         }
051326:         createControlPanel();
051327:         globalThis.__MCN_BOOT_MARK__?.('mission-control-mounted');
051328:         if (readAllyStealPendingState()) {
051329:             updateStatusBox(
051330:                 'Ally Steal: dispatch refresh detected. Resuming confirmation...'
051331:             );
051332:             setTimeout(
051333:                 function() {
051334:                     resumeAllyStealAfterDispatchRefresh(
051335:                         'mission initialize after reload'
051336:                     );
051337:                 },
051338:                 150
051339:             );
051340:             return;
051341:         }
051342:         if (
051343:             readAutoAdvanceAfterDispatchState()
051344:         ) {
051345:             updateStatusBox(
051346:                 'Dispatch & Share transition detected. Confirming the next mission...'
051347:             );
051348:             setTimeout(
051349:                 function() {
051350:                     resumeAutoAdvanceAfterDispatch(
051351:                         'mission initialize after reload'
051352:                     );
051353:                 },
051354:                 150
051355:             );
051356:             return;
051357:         }
051358:         if (!autoModeRunning) {
051359:             if (mfDebugEnabled) {
051360:                 debugLog(
051361:                     'STOPPED MISSION IDLE',
051362:                     'The complete vehicle list remains collapsed until a manual mission tool requests it.'
051363:                 );
051364:             }
051365:             return;
051366:         }
051367:         requireMissionUpdateFirstPass(
051368:             'mission panel initialized'
051369:         );
051370:         if (mfDebugEnabled) {
051371:             debugLog(
051372:                 'UPDATE FIRST GATE',
051373:                 'Deferred automatic full vehicle-list loading until the mission update precheck completes.'
051374:             );
051375:         }
051376:     }
051377:     function getVehicleCheckboxListSignature() {
051378:         const selectionDocument =
051379:             getVehicleSelectionDocument();
051380:         let boxes = [];
051381:         let vehicleRows = 0;
051382:         try {
051383:             boxes = selectionDocument.querySelectorAll(
051384:                 'input.vehicle_checkbox'
051385:             );
051386:             vehicleRows =
051387:                 selectionDocument.querySelectorAll(
051388:                     'tr.vehicle_select_table_tr, #vehicle_table tr[vehicle_id], #vehicle_table tr[data-vehicle-id]'
051389:                 ).length;
051390:         } catch (_error) {}
051391:         const sampleIndexes = new Set();
051392:         const edgeCount = Math.min(10, boxes.length);
051393:         for (let index = 0; index < edgeCount; index += 1) {
051394:             sampleIndexes.add(index);
051395:             sampleIndexes.add(boxes.length - 1 - index);
051396:         }
051397:         const midpoint = Math.floor(boxes.length / 2);
051398:         for (let offset = -2; offset <= 2; offset += 1) {
051399:             if (midpoint + offset >= 0 && midpoint + offset < boxes.length) {
